  "content": "\"\"\"LlamaCppProvider: InferenceProvider using llama-cpp-python with LoRA support.\n\nLoads GGUF models via llama_cpp.Llama with optional LoRA adapter paths.\nDesigned for Apple Silicon (Metal) local inference where adapter hot-loading\nis needed — Ollama cannot load LoRA adapters, and vLLM requires a server.\n\nIMPORTANT: llama_cpp is imported inside method bodies per INFRA-05 pattern\nso that this module is importable in CPU-only CI without llama-cpp-python.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom inference.provider import GenerationResult, InferenceProvider\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass LlamaCppProvider(InferenceProvider):\n    \"\"\"InferenceProvider backed by llama-cpp-python with native LoRA support.\n\n    Unlike OllamaProvider, this loads GGUF models directly and can apply\n    LoRA adapters at load time. Unlike VLLMProvider, no server is needed.\n\n    The model is loaded lazily on first generate() call. When an adapter\n    is loaded, the model is reloaded with the LoRA path applied.\n\n    Args:\n        model_path: Path to the GGUF model file.\n        n_ctx: Context window size. Default: 4096.\n        n_gpu_layers: Layers to offload to GPU (-1 = all). Default: -1.\n\n    Example:\n        >>> provider = LlamaCppProvider(model_path=\"/models/qwen2.5-coder-1.5b.gguf\")\n        >>> result = await provider.generate(\"def hello\", model=\"ignored\")\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        model_path: str | None = None,\n        n_ctx: int = 4096,\n        n_gpu_layers: int = -1,\n    ) -> None:\n        self._model_path = model_path or \"\"\n        self._n_ctx = n_ctx\n        self._n_gpu_layers = n_gpu_layers\n        self._llm: Any = None\n        self._current_lora: str | None = None\n        self._loaded_adapters: dict[str, str] = {}  # id -> path\n\n    def _ensure_model(self, lora_path: str | None = None) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Load or reload the GGUF model, optionally with a LoRA adapter.\n\n        Args:\n            lora_path: Path to LoRA adapter file. If different from current,\n                triggers a model reload.\n        \"\"\"\n        if self._llm is not None and lora_path == self._current_lora:\n            return\n\n        from llama_cpp import Llama  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n        if self._llm is not None:\n            del self._llm\n            self._llm = None\n\n        logger.info(\n            \"Loading GGUF model: %s (lora=%s, n_ctx=%d, n_gpu_layers=%d)\",\n            self._model_path,\n            lora_path,\n            self._n_ctx,\n            self._n_gpu_layers,\n        )\n\n        kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {\n            \"model_path\": self._model_path,\n            \"n_ctx\": self._n_ctx,\n            \"n_gpu_layers\": self._n_gpu_layers,\n            \"verbose\": False,\n        }\n        if lora_path:\n            kwargs[\"lora_path\"] = lora_path\n\n        self._llm = Llama(**kwargs)\n        self._current_lora = lora_path\n\n    async def generate(\n        self,\n        prompt: str,\n        model: str,\n        adapter_id: str | None = None,\n        max_tokens: int = 1024,\n    ) -> GenerationResult:\n        \"\"\"Generate text using llama-cpp-python with optional LoRA adapter.\n\n        Args:\n            prompt: The input prompt.\n            model: Ignored (model is set at construction via model_path).\n            adapter_id: LoRA adapter ID to apply. Must be loaded first.\n            max_tokens: Maximum tokens to generate.\n\n        Returns:\n            GenerationResult with generated text and metadata.\n        \"\"\"\n        lora_path: str | None = None\n        if adapter_id and adapter_id in self._loaded_adapters:\n            lora_path = self._loaded_adapters[adapter_id]\n\n        self._ensure_model(lora_path=lora_path)\n\n        response = self._llm(  # type: ignore[union-attr]\n            prompt,\n            max_tokens=max_tokens,\n            stop=[\"```\\n\", \"\\n\\n\\n\"],\n        )\n\n        text = response[\"choices\"][0][\"text\"]\n        token_count = response[\"usage\"][\"total_tokens\"]\n        finish_reason = response[\"choices\"][0].get(\"finish_reason\", \"stop\")\n\n        return GenerationResult(\n            text=text,\n            model=Path(self._model_path).stem,\n            adapter_id=adapter_id,\n            token_count=token_count,\n            finish_reason=finish_reason,\n        )\n\n    async def load_adapter(self, adapter_id: str, adapter_path: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Register a LoRA adapter for use during generation.\n\n        The adapter is applied on next generate() call by reloading the model\n        with the LoRA path. llama-cpp-python applies LoRA at model load time.\n\n        Args:\n            adapter_id: Unique name for the adapter.\n            adapter_path: Filesystem path to the LoRA adapter weights (GGUF format).\n        \"\"\"\n        self._loaded_adapters[adapter_id] = adapter_path\n        logger.info(\"Registered adapter %s -> %s\", adapter_id, adapter_path)\n\n    async def unload_adapter(self, adapter_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Remove a registered LoRA adapter.\n\n        If the currently active adapter is unloaded, the model will reload\n        without it on the next generate() call.\n\n        Args:\n            adapter_id: The adapter name to remove.\n        \"\"\"\n        if adapter_id in self._loaded_adapters:\n            was_active = (\n                self._current_lora == self._loaded_adapters[adapter_id]\n            )\n            del self._loaded_adapters[adapter_id]\n            if was_active:\n                self._current_lora = None  # Force reload without adapter\n            logger.info(\"Unloaded adapter %s\", adapter_id)\n\n    async def list_adapters(self) -> list[str]:\n        \"\"\"List all registered LoRA adapter IDs.\n\n        Returns:\n            Sorted list of registered adapter IDs.\n        \"\"\"\n        return sorted(self._loaded_adapters.keys())\n"
}

  "new_string": "def load_pretrained(\n    checkpoint_path: str,\n    device: str = \"cpu\",\n    **kwargs: Any,\n) -> Any:\n    \"\"\"Load a pretrained DocToLoraHypernetwork from a checkpoint.\n\n    Args:\n        checkpoint_path: Path to the .pt checkpoint file.\n        device: Device to load onto ('cpu', 'cuda', 'mps'). Default: 'cpu'.\n        **kwargs: Override constructor args (input_dim, num_latents, etc.).\n\n    Returns:\n        DocToLoraHypernetwork nn.Module loaded with pretrained weights.\n    \"\"\"\n    import torch  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n    checkpoint = torch.load(checkpoint_path, map_location=device, weights_only=True)\n\n    # Extract constructor args from checkpoint or use defaults/overrides\n    ctor_args = checkpoint.get(\"hypernetwork_config\", {})\n    ctor_args.update(kwargs)\n    if \"input_dim\" not in ctor_args:\n        ctor_args[\"input_dim\"] = 32000\n\n    model = DocToLoraHypernetwork(**ctor_args)\n    model.load_state_dict(checkpoint[\"model_state_dict\"])\n    model = model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n    return model\n\n\ndef trajectory_to_tokens(\n    trajectory_text: str,\n    vocab_size: int = 32000,\n    max_length: int = 2048,\n) -> \"torch.Tensor\":\n    \"\"\"Encode trajectory text as token IDs for the hypernetwork.\n\n    Uses a simple hash-based tokenization (character trigrams mapped to\n    vocab indices). This is intentionally simple — the hypernetwork learns\n    its own embedding, so exact tokenization doesn't matter as long as\n    it's consistent.\n\n    Args:\n        trajectory_text: Text to encode (plan, code diffs, test results, etc.).\n        vocab_size: Size of the hypernetwork's embedding vocabulary.\n        max_length: Maximum sequence length (truncates or pads).\n\n    Returns:\n        Token ID tensor of shape (1, max_length) ready for hypernetwork forward().\n    \"\"\"\n    import torch  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n    tokens: list[int] = []\n    for i in range(0, len(trajectory_text) - 2):\n        trigram = trajectory_text[i : i + 3]\n        token_id = hash(trigram) % vocab_size\n        tokens.append(token_id)\n\n    # Pad or truncate to max_length\n    if len(tokens) < max_length:\n        tokens.extend([0] * (max_length - len(tokens)))\n    else:\n        tokens = tokens[:max_length]\n\n    return torch.tensor([tokens], dtype=torch.long)\n\n\ndef generate_adapter(\n    hypernetwork: Any,\n    trajectory_text: str,\n    output_dir: str,\n    base_model_id: str,\n    vocab_size: int = 32000,\n    max_length: int = 2048,\n    device: str = \"cpu\",\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"End-to-end: encode trajectory, run hypernetwork, save adapter.\n\n    Args:\n        hypernetwork: A DocToLoraHypernetwork instance.\n        trajectory_text: Text to encode into the adapter.\n        output_dir: Directory to save the adapter files.\n        base_model_id: HuggingFace model ID of the base model.\n        vocab_size: Vocabulary size for tokenization.\n        max_length: Max token sequence length.\n        device: Device for tensor operations.\n\n    Returns:\n        Path to the saved adapter directory.\n    \"\"\"\n    import torch  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n    tokens = trajectory_to_tokens(trajectory_text, vocab_size, max_length)\n    tokens = tokens.to(device)\n\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        weights = hypernetwork(tokens)\n\n    save_hypernetwork_adapter(weights, output_dir, base_model_id)\n    return output_dir\n\n\ndef save_hypernetwork_adapter("
}

execute_node, generate_node, reflect_node, save_trajectory_node\nfrom .state import RuneState\n\n\ndef should_retry(state: RuneState) -> Literal[\"generate\", \"save_trajectory\"]:\n    \"\"\"Route from reflect: retry if attempts remain and tests failed, else save.\n\n    This is a REAL implementation, not a stub:\n    - If tests passed -> save_trajectory (success)\n    - If attempt_count >= max_attempts -> save_trajectory (exhausted)\n    - Otherwise -> generate (retry)\n\n    Args:\n        state: Current agent state after reflection.\n\n    Returns:\n        Next node name: 'generate' to retry or 'save_trajectory' to finish.\n\n    Example:\n        >>> state = {\"tests_passed\": False, \"attempt_count\": 0, \"max_attempts\": 3}\n        >>> should_retry(state)\n        'generate'\n        >>> state2 = {\"tests_passed\": True, \"attempt_count\": 1, \"max_attempts\": 3}\n        >>> should_retry(state2)\n        'save_trajectory'\n    \"\"\"\n    if state[\"tests_passed\"]:\n        return \"save_trajectory\"\n    if state[\"attempt_count\"] >= state[\"max_attempts\"]:\n        return \"save_trajectory\"\n    return \"generate\"\n\n\ndef create_graph() -> Any:\n    \"\"\"Create and compile the Rune agent workflow graph.\n\n    The graph follows this flow:\n    START -> generate -> execute -> reflect -> [should_retry]\n        -> generate (retry) OR save_trajectory -> END\n\n    Returns:\n        Compiled StateGraph ready for execution.\n\n    Example:\n        >>> graph = create_graph()\n        >>> graph is not None\n        True\n    \"\"\"\n    workflow = StateGraph(RuneState)\n\n    workflow.add_node(\"generate\", generate_node)\n    workflow.add_node(\"execute\", execute_node)\n    workflow.add_node(\"reflect\", reflect_node)\n    workflow.add_node(\"save_trajectory\", save_trajectory_node)\n\n    workflow.add_edge(START, \"generate\")\n    workflow.add_edge(\"generate\", \"execute\")\n    workflow.add_edge(\"execute\", \"reflect\")\n    workflow.add_conditional_edges(\"reflect\", should_retry)\n    workflow.add_edge(\"save_trajectory\", END)\n\n    return workflow.compile()\n\n\n# Singleton instance for reuse\n_graph = None\n\n\ndef get_graph() -> Any:\n    \"\"\"Get or create the compiled graph instance.\n\n    Returns:\n        Compiled StateGraph instance.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _graph\n    if _graph is None:\n        _graph = create_graph()\n    return _graph",
  "new_string": "\"\"\"LangGraph workflow for the Rune coding agent recursive loop.\n\nSupports two modes:\n1. Standard loop (create_graph): generate → execute → reflect → retry/save\n2. Single iteration (create_single_iteration_graph): generate → execute →\n   reflect → END. Used by the outer rune_runner iteration loop where the\n   hypernetwork produces a new adapter between iterations.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Any, Literal\n\nfrom langgraph.graph import END, START, StateGraph\n\nfrom .nodes import execute_node, generate_node, reflect_node, save_trajectory_node\nfrom .state import RuneState\n\n\ndef should_retry(state: RuneState) -> Literal[\"generate\", \"save_trajectory\"]:\n    \"\"\"Route from reflect: retry if attempts remain and tests failed, else save.\n\n    This is a REAL implementation, not a stub:\n    - If tests passed -> save_trajectory (success)\n    - If attempt_count >= max_attempts -> save_trajectory (exhausted)\n    - Otherwise -> generate (retry)\n\n    Args:\n        state: Current agent state after reflection.\n\n    Returns:\n        Next node name: 'generate' to retry or 'save_trajectory' to finish.\n\n    Example:\n        >>> state = {\"tests_passed\": False, \"attempt_count\": 0, \"max_attempts\": 3}\n        >>> should_retry(state)\n        'generate'\n        >>> state2 = {\"tests_passed\": True, \"attempt_count\": 1, \"max_attempts\": 3}\n        >>> should_retry(state2)\n        'save_trajectory'\n    \"\"\"\n    if state[\"tests_passed\"]:\n        return \"save_trajectory\"\n    if state[\"attempt_count\"] >= state[\"max_attempts\"]:\n        return \"save_trajectory\"\n    return \"generate\"\n\n\ndef create_graph() -> Any:\n    \"\"\"Create and compile the Rune agent workflow graph.\n\n    The graph follows this flow:\n    START -> generate -> execute -> reflect -> [should_retry]\n        -> generate (retry) OR save_trajectory -> END\n\n    Returns:\n        Compiled StateGraph ready for execution.\n\n    Example:\n        >>> graph = create_graph()\n        >>> graph is not None\n        True\n    \"\"\"\n    workflow = StateGraph(RuneState)\n\n    workflow.add_node(\"generate\", generate_node)\n    workflow.add_node(\"execute\", execute_node)\n    workflow.add_node(\"reflect\", reflect_node)\n    workflow.add_node(\"save_trajectory\", save_trajectory_node)\n\n    workflow.add_edge(START, \"generate\")\n    workflow.add_edge(\"generate\", \"execute\")\n    workflow.add_edge(\"execute\", \"reflect\")\n    workflow.add_conditional_edges(\"reflect\", should_retry)\n    workflow.add_edge(\"save_trajectory\", END)\n\n    return workflow.compile()\n\n\ndef create_single_iteration_graph() -> Any:\n    \"\"\"Create a graph for one iteration: generate → execute → reflect → END.\n\n    Used by rune_runner's outer iteration loop. Each iteration runs one\n    generate/execute/reflect cycle, then returns control to the outer loop\n    which runs the hypernetwork to produce a new adapter for the next iteration.\n\n    Returns:\n        Compiled StateGraph for single-iteration execution.\n    \"\"\"\n    workflow = StateGraph(RuneState)\n\n    workflow.add_node(\"generate\", generate_node)\n    workflow.add_node(\"execute\", execute_node)\n    workflow.add_node(\"reflect\", reflect_node)\n\n    workflow.add_edge(START, \"generate\")\n    workflow.add_edge(\"generate\", \"execute\")\n    workflow.add_edge(\"execute\", \"reflect\")\n    workflow.add_edge(\"reflect\", END)\n\n    return workflow.compile()\n\n\n# Singleton instance for reuse\n_graph = None\n\n\ndef get_graph() -> Any:\n    \"\"\"Get or create the compiled graph instance.\n\n    Returns:\n        Compiled StateGraph instance.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _graph\n    if _graph is None:\n        _graph = create_graph()\n    return _graph"
}

uv run scripts/rune_runner.py --project \"...\" --model-path /path/to/model.gguf\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport asyncio\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport {state.get('tests_passed', False)}\")\n    parts.append(f\"exit_code: {state.get('exit_code', -1)}\")\n\n    return \"\\n\\n\".join(parts)\n\n\nasync def run_iteration(\n    graph: Any,\n    project_prompt: str,\n    adapter_id: str | None,\n    session_id: str,\n    iteration: int,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Run one iteration of the agent loop.\n\n    Args:\n        graph: Compiled \"stderr\": \"\",\n        \"exit_code\": 0,\n        \"tests_passed\": False,\n        \"trajectory\": [],\n        \"outcome\": None,\n    }\n\n    return await graph.ainvoke(initial_state)\n\n\ndef hypernetwork to produce a new adapter from trajectory.\n\n    If no checkpoint is available, logs a warning and returns None\n    (the system falls back to base model inference).\n\n    Args:\n        trajectory_text: Accumulated trajectory to encode.\n        output_dir: Where to save the adapter.\n        base_model_id: Base model identifier.\n        checkpoint_path: Path to pretrained hypernetwork checkpoint.\n        device: Device for computation ('cpu', 'mps', 'cuda').\n\n    Returns:\n        Path to saved adapter directory, or None if hypernetwork unavailable.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not checkpoint_path or not Path(checkpoint_path).exists():\n        logger.warning(\n            \"No hypernetwork checkpoint at %s — skipping adapter generation\",\n            checkpoint_path,\n        )\n        return None\n\n    try:\n        from model_training.hypernetwork import (\n            generate_adapter,\n            load_pretrained,\n        )\n\n        hypernetwork = load_pretrained(checkpoint_path, device=device)\n        return generate_adapter(\n            hypernetwork=hypernetwork,\n            trajectory_text=trajectory_text,\n            output_dir=output_dir,\n            base_model_id=base_model_id,\n            device=device,\n        )\n    except Exception:\n        logger.exception(\"Hypernetwork adapter generation failed\")\n        return None\n\n\nasync def run_project(\n    project_prompt: str,\n    max_iterations: int = 10,\n    checkpoint_path: str | None = None,\n    base_model_id: str = DEFAULT_BASE_MODEL,\n    device: str = \"cpu\",\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Run the full project lifecycle as an iteration state machine.\n\n    Args:\n        project_prompt: Natural language project definition.\n        max_iterations: Maximum number of iterations.\n        checkpoint_path: Path to pretrained hypernetwork checkpoint.\n        base_model_id: HuggingFace model ID of the base model.\n        device: accumulated_trajectory += f\"=== Bootstrap ===\\n{methodology}\\n\\n\"\n\n    bootstrap_adapter_dir = str(adapter_dir / \"iter0_methodology\")\n    adapter_path = run_hypernetwork(\n        trajectory_text=accumulated_trajectory,\n        output_dir=bootstrap_adapter_dir,\n        base_model_id=base_model_id,\n        checkpoint_path=checkpoint_path,\n        device=device,\n    )\n    if adapter_path:\n        current_adapter_id = \"methodology\"\n        current_adapter_path = adapter_path\n        # Register adapter with the provider\n        from inference import     )\n\n        # Run one iteration with constant project prompt\n        state = await run_iteration(\n            graph=graph,\n            project_prompt=project_prompt,\n            adapter_id=current_adapter_id,\n            session_id=session_id,\n            iteration=iteration,\n        )\n\n        # Record result\n        result = {\n            \"iteration\": iteration,\n            \"adapter_id\": current_adapter_id,\n            \"tests_passed\": state.get(\"tests_passed\", False),\n            \"exit_code\": state.get(\"exit_code\", -1),\n            \"generated_code\": state.get(\"generated_code\", \"\"),\n        }\n        iteration_results.append(result)\n\n        # Build trajectory from this iteration's output\n        iter_trajectory = _build_trajectory_text(state, iteration)\n        accumulated_trajectory += iter_trajectory + \"\\n\\n\"\n\n        # Check if project is complete (tests pass)\n        if   iter_adapter_dir = str(adapter_dir / f\"iter{iteration}\")\n        new_adapter_path = run_hypernetwork(\n            trajectory_text=accumulated_trajectory,\n            output_dir=iter_adapter_dir,\n            base_model_id=base_model_id,\n            checkpoint_path=checkpoint_path,\n            device=device,\n        )\n\n        if new_adapter_path:\n            new_adapter_id = f\"progress_v{iteration}\"\n            from inference import get_provider\n\n            provider = get_provider()\n            # Unload previous adapter if any\n            if if args.model_path:\n        os.environ[\"INFERENCE_PROVIDER\"] = \"llamacpp\"\n        os.environ[\"LLAMACPP_MODEL_PATH\"] = args.model_path\n    elif \"INFERENCE_PROVIDER\" not in os.environ:\n logger.info(\"Session: %s\", result[\"session_id\"])\n    logger.info(\"Iterations: %d\", result[\"total_iterations\"])\n    logger.info(\"Final tests passed: %s\", result[\"final_tests_passed\"])\n {
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/docs/rune-methodology.md",
  "content": "# Rune Project Methodology\n\nYou are an autonomous coding agent. Follow this methodology to complete software projects. Your output at each phase determines what happens next — be precise and structured.\n\n## Phase 1: Decomposition\n\nGiven a project description, produce a numbered task list with:\n\n1. Each task has a clear deliverable (a function, class, or module)\n2. Dependencies between tasks are explicit (task N depends on task M)\n3. Tasks are ordered so dependencies come first\n4. Each task includes acceptance criteria (what tests must pass)\n\nOutput format:\n```\nPLAN:\n1. [task name] - [deliverable description]\n   Depends on: none\n   Tests: [describe what to test]\n2. [task name] - [deliverable description]\n   Depends on: 1\n   Tests: [describe what to test]\n...\nEND_PLAN\n```\n\n## Phase 2: Implementation\n\nFor each task in order, generate:\n\n1. The implementation code (functions, classes, modules)\n2. A test suite that validates the acceptance criteria\n3. Code must be self-contained — include all imports\n\nOutput format:\n```python\n# === IMPLEMENTATION ===\n[your code here]\n\n# === TESTS ===\n[pytest-style test functions here]\n```\n\n## Phase 3: Validation\n\nAfter generating code, it will be executed in a sandbox:\n\n- If tests pass: move to the next task\n- If tests fail: analyze the error output and generate a fix\n- Focus on the error message and traceback to identify the issue\n- Do not repeat the same mistake — each fix should address the specific failure\n\n## Phase 4: Integration\n\nAfter all tasks are complete:\n\n1. Combine all modules into a cohesive package\n2. Write integration tests that exercise cross-module workflows\n3. Run the full test suite\n4. Fix any integration issues\n\n## Output Conventions\n\n- Always wrap code in ```python blocks\n- Include type hints on all function signatures\n- Use descriptive function and variable names\n- Keep functions focused — one responsibility per function\n- Tests use assert statements or pytest conventions\n"
}

  "old_string": "async def agent_supervisor(\n    agent_id: str,\n    tasks: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    checkpoint_db: SwarmCheckpointDB,\n    training_queue: asyncio.Queue[TrainingRequest],\n    dry_run: bool = False,\n    max_failures: int = 5,\n    max_attempts: int = 5,\n    shutdown_event: asyncio.Event | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Tier-2 supervisor for a single swarm agent.\n\n    Processes tasks via the LangGraph agent loop (generate → execute →\n    reflect → retry). Each task is a coding problem with a description\n    and test suite. The LLM generates code, the sandbox runs it against\n    tests, and the loop retries on failure up to max_attempts.\n\n    Args:\n        agent_id: Unique identifier for this agent.\n        tasks: List of task dicts with task_id, task_description, test_suite.\n        checkpoint_db: Checkpoint DB for dedup and recovery.\n        training_queue: Queue for submitting training requests.\n        dry_run: If True, skip actual execution.\n        max_failures: Consecutive failures before retiring.\n        max_attempts: Max LangGraph retry attempts per task.\n        shutdown_event: Event to signal graceful shutdown.\n\n    Returns:\n        Summary dict with completed/failed/skipped counts and results.\n    \"\"\"\n    graph = create_graph()\n    completed = 0\n    failed = 0\n    skipped = 0\n    consecutive_failures = 0\n    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n\n    for task in tasks:\n        if shutdown_event and shutdown_event.is_set():\n            break\n\n        task_hash = task.get(\"task_id\", str(uuid.uuid4()))\n\n        if checkpoint_db.is_completed(task_hash):\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n\n        checkpoint_db.mark_running(task_hash, agent_id)\n\n        if dry_run:\n            checkpoint_db.mark_completed(task_hash, \"dry-run\")\n            completed += 1\n            consecutive_failures = 0\n            continue\n\n        try:\n            session_id = f\"swarm-{agent_id}-{task_hash[:8]}\"\n            task_type = task.get(\"task_type\", \"function\")\n\n            # Run the full LangGraph agent loop\n            final_state = await graph.ainvoke({\n                \"task_description\": task[\"task_description\"],\n                \"task_type\": task_type,\n                \"test_suite\": task[\"test_suite\"],\n                \"adapter_ids\": [],\n                \"session_id\": session_id,\n                \"attempt_count\": 0,\n                \"max_attempts\": max_attempts,\n                \"generated_code\": \"\",\n                \"stdout\": \"\",\n                \"stderr\": \"\",\n                \"exit_code\": 0,\n                \"tests_passed\": False,\n                \"trajectory\": [],\n                \"outcome\": None,\n            })\n\n            outcome = final_state[\"outcome\"]\n            task_result = {\n                \"task_id\": task_hash,\n                \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n                \"outcome\": outcome,\n                \"attempts\": final_state[\"attempt_count\"],\n                \"generated_code\": final_state[\"generated_code\"],\n            }\n            results.append(task_result)\n\n            if outcome == \"success\":\n                checkpoint_db.mark_completed(task_hash, \"success\")\n                completed += 1\n                consecutive_failures = 0\n                await training_queue.put(\n                    TrainingRequest(session_id=session_id, task_type=task_type)\n                )\n                logger.info(\n                    \"Agent %s solved task %s in %d attempts\",\n                    agent_id, task_hash, final_state[\"attempt_count\"],\n                )\n            else:\n                checkpoint_db.mark_failed(task_hash, agent_id)\n                failed += 1\n                consecutive_failures += 1\n                logger.warning(\n                    \"Agent %s exhausted on task %s after %d attempts\",\n                    agent_id, task_hash, final_state[\"attempt_count\"],\n                )\n        except Exception:\n            logger.exception(\"Agent %s failed on task %s\", agent_id, task_hash)\n            checkpoint_db.mark_failed(task_hash, agent_id)\n            failed += 1\n            consecutive_failures += 1\n\n        if consecutive_failures >= max_failures:\n            logger.warning(\n                \"Agent %s retiring after %d consecutive failures\",\n                agent_id,\n                max_failures,\n            )\n            break\n\n        # Backoff on failure\n        if consecutive_failures > 0:\n            await asyncio.sleep(min(2**consecutive_failures, 30))\n\n    return {\n        \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n        \"completed\": completed,\n        \"failed\": failed,\n        \"skipped\": skipped,\n        \"results\": results,\n    }",
  "new_string": "async def agent_supervisor(\n    agent_id: str,\n    tasks: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    checkpoint_db: SwarmCheckpointDB,\n    training_queue: asyncio.Queue[TrainingRequest],\n    dry_run: bool = False,\n    max_failures: int = 5,\n    max_attempts: int = 5,\n    shutdown_event: asyncio.Event | None = None,\n    use_iteration_loop: bool = False,\n    hypernetwork_checkpoint: str | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Tier-2 supervisor for a single swarm agent.\n\n    Processes tasks via the LangGraph agent loop (generate → execute →\n    reflect → retry). Each task is a coding problem with a description\n    and test suite. The LLM generates code, the sandbox runs it against\n    tests, and the loop retries on failure up to max_attempts.\n\n    When use_iteration_loop=True, uses rune_runner's hypernetwork iteration\n    loop instead of the standard LangGraph retry loop. Each agent runs its\n    own adapter chain.\n\n    Args:\n        agent_id: Unique identifier for this agent.\n        tasks: List of task dicts with task_id, task_description, test_suite.\n        checkpoint_db: Checkpoint DB for dedup and recovery.\n        training_queue: Queue for submitting training requests.\n        dry_run: If True, skip actual execution.\n        max_failures: Consecutive failures before retiring.\n        max_attempts: Max LangGraph retry attempts per task.\n        shutdown_event: Event to signal graceful shutdown.\n        use_iteration_loop: Use hypernetwork iteration loop per task.\n        hypernetwork_checkpoint: Path to pretrained hypernetwork .pt file.\n\n    Returns:\n        Summary dict with completed/failed/skipped counts and results.\n    \"\"\"\n    graph = create_graph()\n    completed = 0\n    failed = 0\n    skipped = 0\n    consecutive_failures = 0\n    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n\n    for task in tasks:\n        if shutdown_event and shutdown_event.is_set():\n            break\n\n        task_hash = task.get(\"task_id\", str(uuid.uuid4()))\n\n        if checkpoint_db.is_completed(task_hash):\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n\n        checkpoint_db.mark_running(task_hash, agent_id)\n\n        if dry_run:\n            checkpoint_db.mark_completed(task_hash, \"dry-run\")\n            completed += 1\n            consecutive_failures = 0\n            continue\n\n        try:\n            session_id = f\"swarm-{agent_id}-{task_hash[:8]}\"\n            task_type = task.get(\"task_type\", \"function\")\n\n            if use_iteration_loop:\n                # Use hypernetwork iteration loop — each agent gets its\n                # own adapter chain for the task\n                iter_result = await run_iteration_project(\n                    project_prompt=task[\"task_description\"],\n                    max_iterations=max_attempts,\n                    checkpoint_path=hypernetwork_checkpoint,\n                )\n                outcome = (\n                    \"success\" if iter_result[\"final_tests_passed\"] else \"exhausted\"\n                )\n                task_result = {\n                    \"task_id\": task_hash,\n                    \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n                    \"outcome\": outcome,\n                    \"attempts\": iter_result[\"total_iterations\"],\n                    \"generated_code\": iter_result[\"iterations\"][-1][\n                        \"generated_code\"\n                    ]\n                    if iter_result[\"iterations\"]\n                    else \"\",\n                }\n            else:\n                # Standard LangGraph retry loop\n                final_state = await graph.ainvoke({\n                    \"task_description\": task[\"task_description\"],\n                    \"task_type\": task_type,\n                    \"test_suite\": task[\"test_suite\"],\n                    \"adapter_ids\": [],\n                    \"session_id\": session_id,\n                    \"attempt_count\": 0,\n                    \"max_attempts\": max_attempts,\n                    \"generated_code\": \"\",\n                    \"stdout\": \"\",\n                    \"stderr\": \"\",\n                    \"exit_code\": 0,\n                    \"tests_passed\": False,\n                    \"trajectory\": [],\n                    \"outcome\": None,\n                })\n\n                outcome = final_state[\"outcome\"]\n                task_result = {\n                    \"task_id\": task_hash,\n                    \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n                    \"outcome\": outcome,\n                    \"attempts\": final_state[\"attempt_count\"],\n                    \"generated_code\": final_state[\"generated_code\"],\n                }\n\n            results.append(task_result)\n\n            if outcome == \"success\":\n                checkpoint_db.mark_completed(task_hash, \"success\")\n                completed += 1\n                consecutive_failures = 0\n                await training_queue.put(\n                    TrainingRequest(session_id=session_id, task_type=task_type)\n                )\n                logger.info(\n                    \"Agent %s solved task %s in %d attempts\",\n                    agent_id, task_hash, task_result[\"attempts\"],\n                )\n            else:\n                checkpoint_db.mark_failed(task_hash, agent_id)\n                failed += 1\n                consecutive_failures += 1\n                logger.warning(\n                    \"Agent %s exhausted on task %s after %d attempts\",\n                    agent_id, task_hash, task_result[\"attempts\"],\n                )\n        except Exception:\n            logger.exception(\"Agent %s failed on task %s\", agent_id, task_hash)\n            checkpoint_db.mark_failed(task_hash, agent_id)\n            failed += 1\n            consecutive_failures += 1\n\n        if consecutive_failures >= max_failures:\n            logger.warning(\n                \"Agent %s retiring after %d consecutive failures\",\n                agent_id,\n                max_failures,\n            )\n            break\n\n        # Backoff on failure\n        if consecutive_failures > 0:\n            await asyncio.sleep(min(2**consecutive_failures, 30))\n\n    return {\n        \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n        \"completed\": completed,\n        \"failed\": failed,\n        \"skipped\": skipped,\n        \"results\": results,\n    }"
}

            consecutive_failures += 1

            await asyncio.sleep(min(2**consecutive_failures, 30))

    return {
        "agent_id": agent_id,
        "completed": completed,
        "failed": failed,
        "skipped": skipped,

"""Fat orchestrator for parallel swarm execution.

Collapses Rune's microservice architecture into a single-process
orchestrator for local hardware. Coordinates agent supervisors,
training pool, evolution worker, and memory watchdog via asyncio.

Usage:
    uv run scripts/swarm.py [--config scripts/swarm_config.yaml] [--agents N] [--hours H] [--dry-run]
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import asyncio
import json
import logging
import sys
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import yaml

# Add scripts dir to path for sibling imports
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))

from shared.checkpoint_db import SwarmCheckpointDB
from shared.hardware import HardwareProbe
from shared.rune_models import SwarmConfig
from shared.sandbox import get_sandbox_backend
from swarm_evolution import evolution_worker
from swarm_workers import TrainingRequest, memory_watchdog, training_pool_manager

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def load_task_pool(task_source: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Load tasks from a JSON file or return empty list.

    Args:
        task_source: Path to a JSON file containing task definitions.

    Returns:
        List of task dicts with at minimum 'task_id' and 'description'.
    """
    path = Path(task_source)
    if path.exists():
        with path.open() as f:
            return json.load(f)
    logger.warning("Task source %s not found, using empty task pool", task_source)
    return []


async def agent_supervisor(
    agent_id: str,
    tasks: list[dict[str, Any]],
    checkpoint_db: SwarmCheckpointDB,
    training_queue: asyncio.Queue[TrainingRequest],
    dry_run: bool = False,
    max_failures: int = 5,
    shutdown_event: asyncio.Event | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Tier-2 supervisor for a single swarm agent.

    Processes tasks from the pool, checking checkpoints for dedup,
    running code via sandbox, and queuing training requests.

    Args:
        agent_id: Unique identifier for this agent.
        tasks: List of task dicts to process.
        checkpoint_db: Checkpoint DB for dedup and recovery.
        training_queue: Queue for submitting training requests.
        dry_run: If True, skip actual execution.
        max_failures: Consecutive failures before retiring.
        shutdown_event: Event to signal graceful shutdown.

    Returns:
        Summary dict with completed/failed/skipped counts.
    """
    completed = 0
    failed = 0
    skipped = 0
    consecutive_failures = 0
    sandbox = get_sandbox_backend()

    for task in tasks:
        if shutdown_event and shutdown_event.is_set():
            break

        task_hash = task.get("task_id", str(uuid.uuid4()))

        if checkpoint_db.is_completed(task_hash):
            skipped += 1
            continue

        checkpoint_db.mark_running(task_hash, agent_id)

        if dry_run:
            checkpoint_db.mark_completed(task_hash, "dry-run")
            completed += 1
            consecutive_failures = 0
            continue

        try:
            code = task.get("prompt", "") + task.get("canonical_solution", "")
            test = task.get("test", "")
            script = code + "\n" + test
            result = sandbox.run(script, timeout=30)

            if result.exit_code == 0 and not result.timed_out:
                checkpoint_db.mark_completed(task_hash, "success")
                completed += 1
                consecutive_failures = 0
                await training_queue.put(
                    TrainingRequest(session_id=task_hash, task_type="swarm")
                )
            else:
                checkpoint_db.mark_failed(task_hash, agent_id)
                failed += 1
                consecutive_failures += 1
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("Agent %s failed on task %s", agent_id, task_hash)
            checkpoint_db.mark_failed(task_hash, agent_id)
            failed += 1
            consecutive_failures += 1

        if consecutive_failures >= max_failures:
            logger.warning(
                "Agent %s retiring after %d consecutive failures",
                agent_id,
                max_failures,
            )
            break

        # Backoff on failure
        if consecutive_failures > 0:
            await asyncio.sleep(min(2**consecutive_failures, 30))

    return {
        "agent_id": agent_id,
        "completed": completed,
        "failed": failed,
        "skipped": skipped,
    }


async def run_swarm(config: SwarmConfig, dry_run: bool = False) -> None:
    """Tier-1 orchestrator — runs the full swarm with asyncio.TaskGroup.

    Args:
        config: Swarm configuration.
        dry_run: If True, skip actual execution and training.
    """
    from adapter_registry import AdapterRegistry
    from sqlmodel import create_engine

    logger.info("Starting swarm with config: %s", config.model_dump())

    # Detect hardware
    probe = HardwareProbe.detect()
    budget = probe.compute_budget()
    logger.info("Hardware: %s, Budget: %s", probe, budget)

    # Initialize registry and checkpoint DB
    registry_engine = create_engine(config.db_url)
    registry = AdapterRegistry(engine=registry_engine)
    checkpoint_engine = create_engine("sqlite:///swarm_checkpoints.db")
    checkpoint_db = SwarmCheckpointDB(checkpoint_engine)

    # Load tasks
    tasks = load_task_pool(config.task_source)
    if not tasks:
        logger.warning("No tasks to process")
        return

    # Shared state
    training_queue: asyncio.Queue[TrainingRequest] = asyncio.Queue()
    shutdown_event = asyncio.Event()
    num_agents = min(config.population_size, budget.max_agents)

    # Partition tasks across agents
    partitions: list[list[dict[str, Any]]] = [[] for _ in range(num_agents)]
    for i, task in enumerate(tasks):
        partitions[i % num_agents].append(task)

    async with asyncio.TaskGroup() as tg:
        # Agent supervisors
        agent_tasks = []
        for i in range(num_agents):
            t = tg.create_task(
                agent_supervisor(
                    agent_id=f"agent-{i}",
                    tasks=partitions[i],
                    checkpoint_db=checkpoint_db,
                    training_queue=training_queue,
                    dry_run=dry_run,
                    shutdown_event=shutdown_event,
                )
            )
            agent_tasks.append(t)

        # Training pool (background)
        tg.create_task(
            training_pool_manager(
                training_queue=training_queue,
                budget=budget,
                db_url=config.db_url,
                shutdown_event=shutdown_event,
            )
        )

        # Evolution worker (background)
        tg.create_task(
            evolution_worker(
                registry=registry,
                interval_seconds=config.evolution_interval,
                shutdown_event=shutdown_event,
            )
        )

        # Memory watchdog (background)
        tg.create_task(
            memory_watchdog(
                budget=budget,
                agents=agent_tasks,
                shutdown_event=shutdown_event,
            )
        )

        # Wait for all agents to finish, then shutdown background tasks
        done = await asyncio.gather(*agent_tasks, return_exceptions=True)
        shutdown_event.set()

        for result in done:
            if isinstance(result, Exception):
                logger.error("Agent failed: %s", result)
            else:
                logger.info("Agent result: %s", result)


def main() -> None:
    """CLI entry point for the swarm orchestrator."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Rune Swarm Orchestrator")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--config",
        default="scripts/swarm_config.yaml",
        help="Path to swarm config YAML",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--agents", type=int, help="Override population_size")
    parser.add_argument("--hours", type=float, help="Max runtime in hours")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Skip execution")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)

    config_path = Path(args.config)
    if config_path.exists():
        raw = yaml.safe_load(config_path.read_text())
        config = SwarmConfig(**raw)
    else:
        config = SwarmConfig()

    if args.agents:
        config.population_size = args.agents

    asyncio.run(run_swarm(config, dry_run=args.dry_run))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

    77→    """Tier-2 supervisor for a single swarm agent.

    79→    Processes tasks via the LangGraph agent loop (generate → execute →

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/scripts/swarm.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Fat orchestrator for parallel swarm execution.\n\nCollapses Rune's microservice architecture into a single-process\norchestrator for local hardware. Coordinates agent supervisors,\ntraining pool, evolution worker, and memory watchdog via asyncio.\n\nUsage:\n    uv run scripts/swarm.py [--config scripts/swarm_config.yaml] [--agents N] [--hours H] [--dry-run]\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport sys\nimport uuid\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport yaml\n\n# Add scripts dir to path for sibling imports\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))\n\nfrom shared.checkpoint_db import SwarmCheckpointDB\nfrom shared.hardware import HardwareProbe\nfrom shared.rune_models import SwarmConfig\nfrom shared.sandbox import get_sandbox_backend\nfrom swarm_evolution import evolution_worker\nfrom swarm_workers import TrainingRequest, memory_watchdog, training_pool_manager\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\ndef load_task_pool(task_source: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Load tasks from a JSON file or return empty list.\n\n    Args:\n        task_source: Path to a JSON file containing task definitions.\n\n    Returns:\n        List of task dicts with at minimum 'task_id' and 'description'.\n    \"\"\"\n    path = Path(task_source)\n    if path.exists():\n        with path.open() as f:\n            return json.load(f)\n    logger.warning(\"Task source %s not found, using empty task pool\", task_source)\n    return []\n\n\nasync def agent_supervisor(\n    agent_id: str,\n    tasks: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    checkpoint_db: SwarmCheckpointDB,\n    training_queue: asyncio.Queue[TrainingRequest],\n    dry_run: bool = False,\n    max_failures: int = 5,\n    shutdown_event: asyncio.Event | None = None,\n    use_iteration_loop: bool = False,\n    hypernetwork_checkpoint: str | None = None,\n    max_iterations: int = 5,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Tier-2 supervisor for a single swarm agent.\n\n    Processes tasks from the pool, checking checkpoints for dedup,\n    running code via sandbox, and queuing training requests.\n\n    When use_iteration_loop=True, uses rune_runner's hypernetwork iteration\n    loop instead of the standard sandbox approach. Each agent runs its own\n    adapter chain per task.\n\n    Args:\n        agent_id: Unique identifier for this agent.\n        tasks: List of task dicts to process.\n        checkpoint_db: Checkpoint DB for dedup and recovery.\n        training_queue: Queue for submitting training requests.\n        dry_run: If True, skip actual execution.\n        max_failures: Consecutive failures before retiring.\n        shutdown_event: Event to signal graceful shutdown.\n        use_iteration_loop: Use hypernetwork iteration loop per task.\n        hypernetwork_checkpoint: Path to pretrained hypernetwork .pt file.\n        max_iterations: Max iterations for iteration loop mode.\n\n    Returns:\n        Summary dict with completed/failed/skipped counts.\n    \"\"\"\n    completed = 0\n    failed = 0\n    skipped = 0\n    consecutive_failures = 0\n    sandbox = get_sandbox_backend()\n\n    for task in tasks:\n        if shutdown_event and shutdown_event.is_set():\n            break\n\n        task_hash = task.get(\"task_id\", str(uuid.uuid4()))\n\n        if checkpoint_db.is_completed(task_hash):\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n\n        checkpoint_db.mark_running(task_hash, agent_id)\n\n        if dry_run:\n            checkpoint_db.mark_completed(task_hash, \"dry-run\")\n            completed += 1\n            consecutive_failures = 0\n            continue\n\n        try:\n            if use_iteration_loop:\n                # Hypernetwork iteration loop — each agent gets its own\n                # adapter chain for the task\n                from rune_runner import run_project as run_iteration_project\n\n                project_prompt = task.get(\n                    \"task_description\", task.get(\"prompt\", \"\")\n                )\n                iter_result = await run_iteration_project(\n                    project_prompt=project_prompt,\n                    max_iterations=max_iterations,\n                    checkpoint_path=hypernetwork_checkpoint,\n                )\n                if iter_result[\"final_tests_passed\"]:\n                    checkpoint_db.mark_completed(task_hash, \"success\")\n                    completed += 1\n                    consecutive_failures = 0\n                    await training_queue.put(\n                        TrainingRequest(\n                            session_id=task_hash, task_type=\"iteration\"\n                        )\n                    )\n                else:\n                    checkpoint_db.mark_failed(task_hash, agent_id)\n                    failed += 1\n                    consecutive_failures += 1\n            else:\n                # Standard sandbox execution\n                code = task.get(\"prompt\", \"\") + task.get(\n                    \"canonical_solution\", \"\"\n                )\n                test = task.get(\"test\", \"\")\n                script = code + \"\\n\" + test\n                result = sandbox.run(script, timeout=30)\n\n                if result.exit_code == 0 and not result.timed_out:\n                    checkpoint_db.mark_completed(task_hash, \"success\")\n                    completed += 1\n                    consecutive_failures = 0\n                    await training_queue.put(\n                        TrainingRequest(\n                            session_id=task_hash, task_type=\"swarm\"\n                        )\n                    )\n                else:\n                    checkpoint_db.mark_failed(task_hash, agent_id)\n                    failed += 1\n                    consecutive_failures += 1\n        except Exception:\n            logger.exception(\"Agent %s failed on task %s\", agent_id, task_hash)\n            checkpoint_db.mark_failed(task_hash, agent_id)\n            failed += 1\n            consecutive_failures += 1\n\n        if consecutive_failures >= max_failures:\n            logger.warning(\n                \"Agent %s retiring after %d consecutive failures\",\n                agent_id,\n                max_failures,\n            )\n            break\n\n        # Backoff on failure\n        if consecutive_failures > 0:\n            await asyncio.sleep(min(2**consecutive_failures, 30))\n\n    return {\n        \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n        \"completed\": completed,\n        \"failed\": failed,\n        \"skipped\": skipped,\n    }\n\n\nasync def run_swarm(config: SwarmConfig, dry_run: bool = False) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Tier-1 orchestrator — runs the full swarm with asyncio.TaskGroup.\n\n    Args:\n        config: Swarm configuration.\n        dry_run: If True, skip actual execution and training.\n    \"\"\"\n    from adapter_registry import AdapterRegistry\n    from sqlmodel import create_engine\n\n    logger.info(\"Starting swarm with config: %s\", config.model_dump())\n\n    # Detect hardware\n    probe = HardwareProbe.detect()\n    budget = probe.compute_budget()\n    logger.info(\"Hardware: %s, Budget: %s\", probe, budget)\n\n    # Initialize registry and checkpoint DB\n    registry_engine = create_engine(config.db_url)\n    registry = AdapterRegistry(engine=registry_engine)\n    checkpoint_engine = create_engine(\"sqlite:///swarm_checkpoints.db\")\n    checkpoint_db = SwarmCheckpointDB(checkpoint_engine)\n\n    # Load tasks\n    tasks = load_task_pool(config.task_source)\n    if not tasks:\n        logger.warning(\"No tasks to process\")\n        return\n\n    # Shared state\n    training_queue: asyncio.Queue[TrainingRequest] = asyncio.Queue()\n    shutdown_event = asyncio.Event()\n    num_agents = min(config.population_size, budget.max_agents)\n\n    # Partition tasks across agents\n    partitions: list[list[dict[str, Any]]] = [[] for _ in range(num_agents)]\n    for i, task in enumerate(tasks):\n        partitions[i % num_agents].append(task)\n\n    use_iteration = bool(config.hypernetwork_checkpoint)\n\n    async with asyncio.TaskGroup() as tg:\n        # Agent supervisors\n        agent_tasks = []\n        for i in range(num_agents):\n            t = tg.create_task(\n                agent_supervisor(\n                    agent_id=f\"agent-{i}\",\n                    tasks=partitions[i],\n                    checkpoint_db=checkpoint_db,\n                    training_queue=training_queue,\n                    dry_run=dry_run,\n                    shutdown_event=shutdown_event,\n                    use_iteration_loop=use_iteration,\n                    hypernetwork_checkpoint=config.hypernetwork_checkpoint,\n                )\n            )\n            agent_tasks.append(t)\n\n        # Training pool (background)\n        tg.create_task(\n            training_pool_manager(\n                training_queue=training_queue,\n                budget=budget,\n                db_url=config.db_url,\n                shutdown_event=shutdown_event,\n            )\n        )\n\n        # Evolution worker (background)\n        tg.create_task(\n            evolution_worker(\n                registry=registry,\n                interval_seconds=config.evolution_interval,\n                shutdown_event=shutdown_event,\n            )\n        )\n\n        # Memory watchdog (background)\n        tg.create_task(\n            memory_watchdog(\n                budget=budget,\n                agents=agent_tasks,\n                shutdown_event=shutdown_event,\n            )\n        )\n\n        # Wait for all agents to finish, then shutdown background tasks\n        done = await asyncio.gather(*agent_tasks, return_exceptions=True)\n        shutdown_event.set()\n\n        for result in done:\n            if isinstance(result, Exception):\n                logger.error(\"Agent failed: %s\", result)\n            else:\n                logger.info(\"Agent result: %s\", result)\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    \"\"\"CLI entry point for the swarm orchestrator.\"\"\"\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Rune Swarm Orchestrator\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--config\",\n        default=\"scripts/swarm_config.yaml\",\n        help=\"Path to swarm config YAML\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--agents\", type=int, help=\"Override population_size\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--hours\", type=float, help=\"Max runtime in hours\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--dry-run\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Skip execution\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)\n\n    config_path = Path(args.config)\n    if config_path.exists():\n        raw = yaml.safe_load(config_path.read_text())\n        config = SwarmConfig(**raw)\n    else:\n        config = SwarmConfig()\n\n    if args.agents:\n        config.population_size = args.agents\n\n    asyncio.run(run_swarm(config, dry_run=args.dry_run))\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

Everything passes. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

## Summary

### Step 1: LlamaCppProvider
- **NEW** `libs/inference/src/inference/llamacpp_provider.py` — `InferenceProvider` using `llama_cpp.Llama` with native LoRA support. Loads GGUF models directly, applies LoRA at model load time, hot-swaps adapters by reloading.
- **EDIT** `libs/inference/src/inference/factory.py` — Added `"llamacpp"` provider type with `LLAMACPP_MODEL_PATH` env var routing
- **EDIT** `libs/inference/src/inference/__init__.py` — Added `LlamaCppProvider` to lazy imports and `__all__`
- **EDIT** `pyproject.toml` — Added `llama-cpp-python>=0.3.0` dep + `llama_cpp` mypy ignore + E402 ignore for scripts

### Step 2: Hypernetwork Iteration State Machine
- **EDIT** `libs/model-training/src/model_training/hypernetwork.py` — Added `load_pretrained()`, `trajectory_to_tokens()`, and `generate_adapter()` for end-to-end trajectory→adapter pipeline with MPS support
- **EDIT** `services/rune-agent/src/rune_agent/graph.py` — Added `create_single_iteration_graph()` (generate→execute→reflect→END) for outer iteration loop
- **NEW** `scripts/rune_runner.py` — The outer iteration loop: bootstrap (methodology→adapter), then iterate (constant prompt + latest adapter → generate → execute → H() → next adapter)

### Step 3: Methodology Document
- **NEW** `docs/rune-methodology.md` — Structured methodology for Phase 1 (decompose), Phase 2 (implement), Phase 3 (validate), Phase 4 (integrate) with output format conventions

### Step 4: Swarm Integration
- **EDIT** `scripts/swarm.py` — Added `use_iteration_loop` and `hypernetwork_checkpoint` params to `agent_supervisor`. When `config.hypernetwork_checkpoint` is set, agents use rune_runner iteration loop per task.
- **EDIT** `libs/shared/src/shared/rune_models.py` — Added `hypernetwork_checkpoint` field to `SwarmConfig`

### Step 5: E2E Usage
```bash
uv run scripts/rune_runner.py --project "Build a Python statistics library with mean, median, mode, stdev, percentile"
```

    import math
    
    class Statistics:
        def __init__(self, data):
            self.data = data
    
        def mean(self):
            return sum(self.data) / len(self.data)
    
        def median(self):
            nums = sorted(self.data)
            n = len(nums)
            mid = n // 2
            if n % 2 == 0:
                return (nums[mid - 1] + nums[mid]) / 2
            else:
                return nums[mid]
    
        def mode(self):
            counts = {}
            for num in self.data:
                if num in counts:
                    counts[num] += 1
                else:
                    counts[num] = 1
            max_count = max(counts.values())
            for num, count in counts.items():
                if count == max_count:
                    return num

The E2E loop works end-to-end. Here's what happened across the 3 iterations:

**Iteration 1** — Model generated code using `numpy` (`np.mean`, `np.median`, `np.ma.mode`). Failed with `exit_code=1` because `numpy` isn't installed in the sandbox.

**Iteration 2** — Model tried `numpy` + `pandas`. Still failed — same dependency issue, plus test assertions used approximate values that didn't match exactly.

**Iteration 3** — Model self-corrected to a **pure Python** implementation using only `math` stdlib. No external deps. Implemented `mean`, `median`, `mode`, `stdev`, `percentile` with its own test suite. **Tests passed** (`exit_code=0`).

Key observations from the trace:
- **Prompt stayed constant** across all 3 iterations (same project definition)
- **No hypernetwork checkpoint** was available, so it ran with base model only (adapters would carry context in production; here the model's stochastic variation found the right approach)
- **Bootstrap phase** completed (methodology loaded, but no adapter generated without checkpoint)
- **Session ID** `rune-c167226d` tracked consistently
- **Adapter directory** created at `~/.rune/adapters/rune-c167226d` (empty since no hypernetwork checkpoint — in production, each iteration would save an adapter there)
- Loop correctly **stopped at iteration 3** when tests passed, rather than running all iterations

You're right. The core issue is threefold:

1. No hypernetwork checkpoint → H() never runs
2. Ollama can't load LoRA adapters → adapter path is dead
3. No provider can load PEFT safetensors adapters natively

The hypernetwork outputs PEFT safetensors, but LlamaCpp expects GGUF LoRA format and Ollama doesn't support adapters at all. I need a `TransformersProvider` that uses HuggingFace transformers + PEFT directly — that's the natural match for PEFT safetensors adapters.

Let me fix this end-to-end.

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/libs/inference/src/inference/transformers_provider.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"TransformersProvider: InferenceProvider using HuggingFace transformers + PEFT.\n\nLoads models via AutoModelForCausalLM and applies LoRA adapters via PEFT.\nThis is the only provider that natively supports PEFT-format adapters\n(safetensors) as output by the hypernetwork.\n\nIMPORTANT: transformers, torch, and peft are imported inside method bodies\nper INFRA-05 pattern so that this module is importable in CPU-only CI.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom inference.provider import GenerationResult, InferenceProvider\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass TransformersProvider(InferenceProvider):\n    \"\"\"InferenceProvider backed by HuggingFace transformers with PEFT LoRA.\n\n    Loads models locally via AutoModelForCausalLM. Adapters are applied\n    via PEFT's PeftModel, which natively reads the safetensors format\n    output by the hypernetwork.\n\n    Args:\n        model_name: HuggingFace model ID or local path.\n        device: Device to load model onto ('cpu', 'mps', 'cuda').\n        torch_dtype: Model dtype ('auto', 'float16', 'bfloat16').\n\n    Example:\n        >>> provider = TransformersProvider(model_name=\"Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B\")\n        >>> result = await provider.generate(\"def hello\", model=\"ignored\")\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        model_name: str = \"\",\n        device: str = \"cpu\",\n        torch_dtype: str = \"auto\",\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Initialize TransformersProvider.\n\n        Args:\n            model_name: HuggingFace model ID or local path.\n            device: Device to load model onto.\n            torch_dtype: Model dtype string.\n        \"\"\"\n        self._model_name = model_name\n        self._device = device\n        self._torch_dtype = torch_dtype\n        self._model: Any = None\n        self._tokenizer: Any = None\n        self._base_model: Any = None\n        self._loaded_adapters: dict[str, str] = {}  # id -> path\n        self._active_adapter: str | None = None\n\n    def _ensure_model(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Load the base model and tokenizer if not already loaded.\"\"\"\n        if self._model is not None:\n            return\n\n        from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n        logger.info(\"Loading model: %s (device=%s)\", self._model_name, self._device)\n\n        self._tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(self._model_name)\n        if self._tokenizer.pad_token is None:\n            self._tokenizer.pad_token = self._tokenizer.eos_token\n\n        self._model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n            self._model_name,\n            torch_dtype=self._torch_dtype,\n        )\n        self._model.to(self._device)\n        self._model.eval()\n        self._base_model = self._model\n        logger.info(\"Model loaded: %s\", self._model_name)\n\n    async def generate(\n        self,\n        prompt: str,\n        model: str,\n        adapter_id: str | None = None,\n        max_tokens: int = 1024,\n    ) -> GenerationResult:\n        \"\"\"Generate text using transformers with optional PEFT adapter.\n\n        Args:\n            prompt: The input prompt.\n            model: Ignored (model is set at construction).\n            adapter_id: LoRA adapter ID to activate. Must be loaded first.\n            max_tokens: Maximum tokens to generate.\n\n        Returns:\n            GenerationResult with generated text and metadata.\n        \"\"\"\n        import torch  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n        self._ensure_model()\n\n        # Switch adapter if needed\n        if adapter_id and adapter_id != self._active_adapter:\n            if adapter_id in self._loaded_adapters:\n                self._activate_adapter(adapter_id)\n        elif not adapter_id and self._active_adapter:\n            self._deactivate_adapter()\n\n        inputs = self._tokenizer(\n            prompt, return_tensors=\"pt\", truncation=True, max_length=2048\n        )\n        inputs = {k: v.to(self._device) for k, v in inputs.items()}\n        input_len = inputs[\"input_ids\"].shape[1]\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            outputs = self._model.generate(\n                **inputs,\n                max_new_tokens=max_tokens,\n                do_sample=True,\n                temperature=0.7,\n                top_p=0.9,\n                pad_token_id=self._tokenizer.pad_token_id,\n            )\n\n        new_tokens = outputs[0][input_len:]\n        text = self._tokenizer.decode(new_tokens, skip_special_tokens=True)\n        total_tokens = outputs.shape[1]\n\n        return GenerationResult(\n            text=text,\n            model=self._model_name,\n            adapter_id=self._active_adapter,\n            token_count=total_tokens,\n            finish_reason=\"stop\",\n        )\n\n    def _activate_adapter(self, adapter_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Activate a loaded PEFT adapter.\"\"\"\n        if adapter_id not in self._loaded_adapters:\n            return\n\n        from peft import PeftModel  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n        adapter_path = self._loaded_adapters[adapter_id]\n\n        if self._active_adapter:\n            # Already has a PEFT wrapper — load additional adapter\n            self._model.load_adapter(adapter_path, adapter_name=adapter_id)\n            self._model.set_adapter(adapter_id)\n        else:\n            # First adapter — wrap base model with PeftModel\n            self._model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(\n                self._base_model, adapter_path, adapter_name=adapter_id\n            )\n            self._model.to(self._device)\n            self._model.eval()\n\n        self._active_adapter = adapter_id\n        logger.info(\"Activated adapter: %s\", adapter_id)\n\n    def _deactivate_adapter(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Deactivate current adapter, revert to base model.\"\"\"\n        if self._active_adapter and self._base_model is not None:\n            self._model = self._base_model\n            self._active_adapter = None\n            logger.info(\"Deactivated adapter, reverted to base model\")\n\n    async def load_adapter(self, adapter_id: str, adapter_path: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Register a PEFT adapter directory for use during generation.\n\n        The adapter directory must contain adapter_model.safetensors and\n        adapter_config.json as output by save_hypernetwork_adapter().\n\n        Args:\n            adapter_id: Unique name for the adapter.\n            adapter_path: Path to the PEFT adapter directory.\n        \"\"\"\n        self._loaded_adapters[adapter_id] = adapter_path\n        logger.info(\"Registered adapter %s -> %s\", adapter_id, adapter_path)\n\n    async def unload_adapter(self, adapter_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Remove a registered adapter.\n\n        Args:\n            adapter_id: The adapter name to remove.\n        \"\"\"\n        if adapter_id in self._loaded_adapters:\n            if self._active_adapter == adapter_id:\n                self._deactivate_adapter()\n            del self._loaded_adapters[adapter_id]\n            logger.info(\"Unloaded adapter %s\", adapter_id)\n\n    async def list_adapters(self) -> list[str]:\n        \"\"\"List all registered adapter IDs.\n\n        Returns:\n            Sorted list of registered adapter IDs.\n        \"\"\"\n        return sorted(self._loaded_adapters.keys())\n"
}

```
     1→"""Provider factory with instance cache for the inference library.
     2→
     3→Selects between VLLMProvider and OllamaProvider based on configuration,
     4→caching instances by (provider_type, base_url) to avoid redundant construction.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→import os
     8→
     9→from inference.provider import InferenceProvider
    10→
    11→# Module-level constants for default values only; actual env var reads happen
    12→# at call time inside get_provider() so monkeypatch.setenv() works in tests.
    13→_DEFAULT_INFERENCE_PROVIDER = "vllm"
    14→_DEFAULT_VLLM_BASE_URL = "http://localhost:8100/v1"
    15→_DEFAULT_OLLAMA_BASE_URL = "http://localhost:11434/v1"
    16→_DEFAULT_LLAMACPP_MODEL_PATH = ""
    17→
    18→_provider_cache: dict[tuple[str, str], InferenceProvider] = {}
    19→
    20→
    21→def _clear_cache() -> None:
    22→    """Clear the provider instance cache.
    23→
    24→    Called by the clear_provider_cache conftest fixture to prevent cache
    25→    state leaking between tests.
    26→    """
    27→    _provider_cache.clear()
    28→
    29→
    30→def get_provider(
    31→    provider_type: str | None = None,
    32→    base_url: str | None = None,
    33→) -> InferenceProvider:
    34→    """Return a cached InferenceProvider for the given backend.
    35→
    36→    Resolves the provider type from the argument or the INFERENCE_PROVIDER
    37→    env var (default: "vllm"). Resolves the base URL from the argument or
    38→    the per-backend env var (VLLM_BASE_URL / OLLAMA_BASE_URL). Instances
    39→    are cached by the (provider_type, base_url) tuple so repeated calls
    40→    with the same arguments return the identical object.
    41→
    42→    Args:
    43→        provider_type: One of "vllm" or "ollama". If None, falls back to
    44→            the INFERENCE_PROVIDER environment variable (default "vllm").
    45→        base_url: Override URL for the backend server. If None, the
    46→            per-backend default env var is used.
    47→
    48→    Returns:
    49→        A cached InferenceProvider instance for the requested backend.
    50→
    51→    Raises:
    52→        ValueError: If provider_type is not "vllm", "ollama", or "llamacpp".
    53→
    54→    Example:
    55→        >>> provider = get_provider("vllm")
    56→        >>> isinstance(provider, VLLMProvider)
    57→        True
    58→    """
    59→    ptype = (
    60→        provider_type
    61→        or os.environ.get("INFERENCE_PROVIDER", _DEFAULT_INFERENCE_PROVIDER)
    62→    ).lower()
    63→
    64→    resolved_url: str
    65→    if ptype == "vllm":
    66→        resolved_url = base_url or os.environ.get(
    67→            "VLLM_BASE_URL", _DEFAULT_VLLM_BASE_URL
    68→        )
    69→    elif ptype == "ollama":
    70→        resolved_url = base_url or os.environ.get(
    71→            "OLLAMA_BASE_URL", _DEFAULT_OLLAMA_BASE_URL
    72→        )
    73→    elif ptype == "llamacpp":
    74→        resolved_url = base_url or os.environ.get(
    75→            "LLAMACPP_MODEL_PATH", _DEFAULT_LLAMACPP_MODEL_PATH
    76→        )
    77→    else:
    78→        raise ValueError(
    79→            f"Unknown provider type: '{ptype}'. "
    80→            "Supported values: 'vllm', 'ollama', 'llamacpp'."
    81→        )
    82→
    83→    cache_key = (ptype, resolved_url)
    84→    if cache_key not in _provider_cache:
    85→        if ptype == "vllm":
    86→            from inference.vllm_provider import VLLMProvider
    87→
    88→            _provider_cache[cache_key] = VLLMProvider(base_url=resolved_url)
    89→        elif ptype == "llamacpp":
    90→            from inference.llamacpp_provider import LlamaCppProvider
    91→
    92→            _provider_cache[cache_key] = LlamaCppProvider(model_path=resolved_url)
    93→        else:
    94→            from inference.ollama_provider import OllamaProvider
    95→
    96→            _provider_cache[cache_key] = OllamaProvider(base_url=resolved_url)
    97→
    98→    return _provider_cache[cache_key]
    99→
   100→
   101→def get_provider_for_step(step_config: dict[str, str]) -> InferenceProvider:
   102→    """Return a cached InferenceProvider configured from a step config dict.
   103→
   104→    Reads "provider" and optionally "base_url" from the step config and
   105→    delegates to get_provider(). Designed for use by the agent loop where
   106→    each pipeline step may specify its own provider and server URL.
   107→
   108→    Args:
   109→        step_config: Dict with optional keys:
   110→            - "provider": Provider type ("vllm" or "ollama").
   111→            - "base_url": Override URL for the backend server.
   112→
   113→    Returns:
   114→        A cached InferenceProvider instance for the step's backend.
   115→
   116→    Raises:
   117→        ValueError: If the provider type in step_config is not supported.
   118→
   119→    Example:
   120→        >>> provider = get_provider_for_step({"provider": "ollama"})
   121→        >>> isinstance(provider, OllamaProvider)
   122→        True
   123→    """
   124→    return get_provider(
   125→        provider_type=step_config.get("provider"),
   126→        base_url=step_config.get("base_url"),
   127→    )
   128→
```

     1→"""Inference provider library for LLM generation and LoRA adapter management.
     2→
     3→Provides a provider-agnostic interface (InferenceProvider) with vLLM and
     4→Ollama backends, a factory for backend selection by configuration, and
     5→structured generation results.
     6→
     7→Provider classes (OllamaProvider, VLLMProvider) are lazily imported to avoid
     8→hard failures when the ``openai`` package is not installed (e.g. in CI).
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
    14→
    15→from inference.exceptions import UnsupportedOperationError
    16→from inference.factory import get_provider, get_provider_for_step
    17→from inference.provider import GenerationResult, InferenceProvider
    18→
    19→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    20→    from inference.llamacpp_provider import LlamaCppProvider
    21→    from inference.ollama_provider import OllamaProvider
    22→    from inference.vllm_provider import VLLMProvider
    23→
    24→__all__ = [
    25→    "GenerationResult",
    26→    "InferenceProvider",
    27→    "LlamaCppProvider",
    28→    "OllamaProvider",
    29→    "UnsupportedOperationError",
    30→    "VLLMProvider",
    31→    "get_provider",
    32→    "get_provider_for_step",
    33→]
    34→
    35→
    36→def __getattr__(name: str) -> object:
    37→    if name == "OllamaProvider":
    38→        from inference.ollama_provider import OllamaProvider
    39→
    40→        return OllamaProvider
    41→    if name == "VLLMProvider":
    42→        from inference.vllm_provider import VLLMProvider
    43→
    44→        return VLLMProvider
    45→    if name == "LlamaCppProvider":
    46→        from inference.llamacpp_provider import LlamaCppProvider
    47→
    48→        return LlamaCppProvider
    49→    raise AttributeError(f"module 'inference' has no attribute {name!r}")
    50→
